### Independent PR #13 integration audit — checkpoint 6: production BLOCKER confirmed statically (2026-09-23)

- **FINDING / BLOCKER FOUND — H-OBJ visible-span termination reads beyond its list.** Exact production head `c2fb4483b92d3d6034ad8c0391e4ba839398917a`, `src/rsp_main.S:1127–1151`, common `next_object -> obj_advance_segment -> obj_window_segment`. When the current visible interval ends at255, OBJ must finish and restore full scissor. It instead reads the next interval's lower-bound byte regardless of whether that interval exists. This is a new H-OBJ traversal defect in the proposed production change, not an inherited general SNES limitation.
- **EVIDENCE / MEASURED binary inspection:** candidate artifact10778627938 contains OBJ next-bound instruction **IMEM0x1A3C = 0x930A0E86** (`lbu t2,WIN_BOUNDS+2(t8)`), followed by compare255 / advance t8+=2 / branch to obj_window_segment. No current-upper-bound termination precedes it. By contrast BG's compiled finish path has the upper-bound248 test at0x1700–0x1704 before its next-bound read at0x1708. Candidate initial DMEM E7C..E9F is zero. Source `calc_windows` emits only [0,255] for disabled W1 or [0,WH0,WH1,255] for normal W1; it does not append the sentinel consumed by this OBJ loop after the last visible span.
- **CONCRETE LEGAL STATE / source-level reproduction:** fresh renderer state, Mode1, OBJ on main, TS=0, TMW=0x10, TSW=0, WOBJSEL=0x02, WH0=64, WH1=191, no prior inverted-window helper call (BG can be absent or unwindowed). The legitimate spans use indices0 and2. After index2 upper255, code reads WIN_BOUNDS+4=0 and advances to index4 instead of finishing. Further iterations reach **index12/E90 and index14/E92, which are the immutable overlay source pointer bytes**, not window bounds. Exact candidate main slot source pointer is0x800B7A80, so index14 supplies bogus bounds0x7A/0x80 and emits an extra scissor at guest x123..128, *inside* the masked middle. The loop likewise overreads immediately after [0,255] when TMW OBJ is enabled but WOBJSEL disables the window.
- **WHAT IT PROVES / SUPPORTED INTERPRETATION:** static control flow and compiled data establish unauthorized traversal and potential drawing within the masked interval, plus repeated object-list/RDP work and possible prolonged/nontermination depending on subsequent DMEM bytes. This suffices to block this exact candidate. **Not dynamically measured:** final framebuffer corruption, hardware stall, precise extra-work count or FPS loss. The small read-only byte model was a bounds walkthrough, not an emulator/hardware test; its later iterations cannot establish timing or all evolving DMEM contents.
- **WHY CURRENT GREEN DOES NOT FALSIFY IT:** H-OBJ guest uses inverted W1 (WOBJSEL03) which writes WIN_BOUNDS+4=255 and ends safely after its middle span. It also windows BG1, which can leave that sentinel in scratch and accidentally mask the defect in other tests. C2/D have OBJ disabled in their controlled workload. WH0-WH3 urgency does not initialize unused window-list bytes and cannot repair the loop.
- **MINIMAL NEXT TEST / not executed:** original diagnostic with main OBJ enabled, **BG absent**, no subscreen/color math, WOBJSEL02, WH0=64/WH1=191; control TMW00, treatment TMW10, cold initialization. Put an opaque sprite across x120..135 and outer controls, compare reference/candidate, and observe t8 at obj_advance_segment: after the second span it must restore scissor without reading index4. A second minimal disabled-window control is WOBJSEL00 with TMW10. These isolate list termination and avoid a preceding inverted BG window seeding a sentinel. Expected proper result: middle sprite masked, outer sprites preserved, bounded traversal; framebuffer timeout is evidence to inspect, not a semantic PASS.
- **DECISION:** exact candidate must not be treated as ready to merge until this concrete generic span-termination defect is addressed and the discriminating case is validated. No implementation performed. Overlay self-DMA architecture itself remains supported; no redesign proposed.

### Independent PR #13 integration audit — FINAL (2026-09-23 UTC)

**Verdict: blocker found.** Applies only to production candidate **`c2fb4483b92d3d6034ad8c0391e4ba839398917a`**, compared with **master `5b7134930a0ca859f6aa24e54102de116948e3ed`** and old candidate84ecafad. PR remains OPEN on `phase4/gate-c-rsp-overlay-clean`. M3/Gate C remains active. The blocker is the included H-OBJ span iterator, **not** the fixed-slot overlay design. The prior “composed validation closed green => strong merge candidate” assessment is **SUPERSEDED as an integration recommendation** by the newly identified missing termination test.

#### 1. Blocking production finding and evidence boundary

**BLOCKER / statically established:** `src/rsp_main.S:1127–1151` and `src/rsp_mode7.S:1096–1120` fail to stop after drawing a visible span whose right bound is255. Their common binary reads WIN_BOUNDS[t8+2] even when the list has ended. `calc_windows` does not guarantee a trailing255 beyond the final visible span. Candidate binary and concrete legal input/cold-DMEM trace are recorded in checkpoint6; the sequence reaches overlay pointers E90/E94 and treats their bytes as scissor coordinates. This is a finite-list contract violation introduced by the H-OBJ path. No new generic-renderer theory or per-game assumption is required.

**Not measured:** actual pixel image, hang duration, DMA busy pattern or FPS degradation for this input. A “hardware crash” claim would overstate the evidence. The malformed additional command sequence and out-of-list read are sufficient to withhold integration of the exact head.

**Next single technical batch:** isolate normal W1 OBJ masking with **no preceding windowed BG**, as specified in checkpoint6. Stop/inspect when a completed span ends255; the next action must be scissor restoration, not index4 traversal. Put the middle sprite atx120..135, preferably OBJ priority0 to expose the first object-list pass, and retain outer controls. Then address only the generic visible-span termination contract in the ordinary implementation workflow. No patch, PR, workflow or experiment was created/executed by this auditor. Do not spend another N64 session revalidating self-DMA before resolving this source-visible blocker.

#### 2. Overlay self-DMA audit — no separate blocker found

**MEASURED:** compiled payload comparison, slot/resident bounds and data-pointer invariants in checkpoint2. Exact candidate's two embedded microcodes and data images equal the C2 package, despite relocated R4300/RDRAM symbols. Candidate regular slot source0x800B7A80 and Mode7 source0x800B9780 are aligned, immutable embedded data below FRAMEBUFFER1=0xA00F2300; they do not overlap mutable framebuffer/tile-cache regions. Destination is IMEM-only [0x13A8,0x1790); resident DMA/wait/loaders are outside it. Startup `main.S:153–170,190–204` waits for its initial uploads before publishing the two DMEM pointers; normal runtime does not use CPU-side renderer replacement.

**SUPPORTED:** every loader uses the serialized blocking DMA helper, then tail-branches to a fixed slot entry. No return PC into overwritten renderer interior, lost live t0 descriptor, shared-vector reset requirement or queued second-SP-DMA producer was found. Common resident code and relevant DMEM ABI agree exactly. The asymmetric ABI break found in C instrumentation is absent here. Capacity is3328B/768B free, not a semantic correctness proof.

#### 3. Mid-frame and section/WH composition

**SUPPORTED:** `ppu.S:215–246,353–408,986–997` records section state including BGMODE; RSP `next_section:222–238` DMAs each64B section, updates k0/k1, and `next_layer:483–500` dispatches against that section's layer chart. Fault stubs work on demand; no once-per-frame overlay latch or end-of-frame-only restore was found. Several section mode changes in one frame follow the same dispatch/load path. Regular/Mode7 entry can fault even before its subsequent BG-enable check, so **do not claim every mode change corresponds to exactly one necessary/useful transfer**. General adaptive sampling of BGMODE remains inherited producer behavior; WH-only urgency does not make every PPU register raster-urgent.

**MEASURED / coverage limit:** old same-frame run35533933132/job106139474289 captures pre-test guestcounter11 and post-test37, two IRQ IDs30/30, final regular slot and changed framebuffer pointer. It does not capture the transient slot or the middle band's pixels. Physical C is invalid for two independently identified diagnostic defects (checkpoints3–4). Therefore physical same-frame execution and pixel fidelity remain **UNKNOWN**, not failed production evidence.

**WH0–WH3:** reconciled `ppu.S` is exactly master. Changed WH values branch to `update_window_frame:419–424` (dirty and clear countdown); equal writes do not. This changes when section snapshots are emitted, not their size/ABI. RSP recomputes OBJ window bounds per priority/section, so there is no frame-cached mask dependency to invalidate after a WH change. **However**, current H-OBJ guest explicitly disables HDMA/IRQ and holds WH constant; its success does not validate simultaneous scanline WH motion and Mode7 switching. More emitted sections can increase the cost of the faulty iterator; urgency neither causes nor fixes its missing termination. No other concrete composition defect was established.

#### 4. Claims, nonblocking risks and rejected explanations

- **MEASURED / recorded hardware authority:** C2 OVM2 SRAM evidence proves the two physical slot signatures with halted/idle SP at steady Mode7 and returned Mode1. D SRAM evidence establishes60/60x5 workload throughput with Road-valid settings. This audit independently inspected their code/package lineage and C2 compiled payloads; it **did not reacquire hardware or independently reread the uploaded raw SRAM files**. Those measurements remain inherited, hash-identified project evidence, not new measurements in this audit.
- **Overclaim rejected:** C2 does not prove every D frame executes a DMA, full transferred-payload integrity by checksum, all same-frame switches, Mode7 pixel fidelity, or RDP quiescence. D does not isolate DMA latency/bus cost or prove all Gate-B corpus results on the composed candidate. Ares/Mupen remain lab authorities for their observations, never physical timing.
- **Overclaim rejected:** composed H-OBJ green covers inverted W1, main-only, static WH, outer-pair masking. It is not general normal/inverted/disabled-window validation, an HDMA interaction proof, or proof that all scissor paths terminate. Existing independent TMW/TSW, W2/combine and Mode7-window limitations remain outside this narrow repair; do not use them as artificial new merge blockers.
- **Nonblocking maintenance risk:** fixed offsets, duplicated resident code and cross-image jumps require compiled invariant checking on any future renderer/instrumentation edit. C's proven ABI drift demonstrates a real failure mode of *future edits*, while candidate invariants currently pass.
- **Nonblocking coverage risk:** multiple physical mid-frame transitions lack a valid direct measurement. Correct the diagnostic epoch and ABI if this coverage is revisited; do not infer failure from C or retry its current binary.
- **Inherited `rdp_send` risk, not a separate PR13 blocker:** candidate `rsp_main.S:1387–1396` is instruction-for-instruction the master routine at1628–1637, including DP_START in the busy-branch delay slot. Overlay self-DMA does not alter DP registers or command-buffer contents. No observed/counterexample interaction specific to the slot mechanism was established. The H-OBJ bug adds erroneous command traffic, but that is already a concrete blocker without assuming this separate potential RDP hazard.
- **REJECTED:** slot overwrites its active loader; candidate cross-image resident addresses drift; reconciled compiled payload differs from C2; C0/0 proves no production mid-frame switching; SP HALT/green CI proves full visual correctness. Each rejection is limited to the evidence actually inspected.
- **SUPERSEDED next-test advice:** checkpoint3's delayed-capture-only experiment is not sufficient because checkpoint4 proves a second C-only ABI defect. Do not reuse it without static ABI reconciliation or use it to motivate a production workaround.

#### 5. Audit completion / persistence

All requested areas reviewed: fixed-slot/self-DMA, mid-frame dispatch and section publication, WH/H-OBJ composition, evidence claims, and unrelated-risk triage. **One production blocker found; two concrete diagnostic-only defects found.** Outstanding uncertainty is dynamic manifestation of the production bounds defect, not whether the missing termination branch exists. The highest-information next step is the isolated normal-W1 OBJ span test; a redesign, additional overlay roadmap or broad hardware retest is not justified.

Only `continuity:docs/CONTINUITY.md` was changed, through incremental verified checkpoints. Runtime/master/candidate/workflows/PR untouched; no merge. Preserve checkpoint history, including the superseded experimental suggestion, so later work does not repeat it.

### 2026-09-24 audit checkpoint 2 — additional H-OBJ boundary defect

- **SUPPORTED INTERPRETATION / concrete remaining production fidelity defect:** master f606cde5 / final candidate f8d55b95, rsp_main.S calc_windows/win_out:1302–1309 and start_objects:939–942 / obj_advance_segment:1146–1151. Legal input: Mode1, main OBJ only(TM10/TS00), TMW10/TSW00, WOBJSEL03 (inverted W1), WH0=WH1=255, opaque OBJ spanning x248..255. Pinned ares17813a3c ares/sfc/ppu/window.cpp Window::run defines window membership inclusively (x>=left && x<=right); inversion masks outside, so x255 must survive. Sodium64 emits WIN_BOUNDS=[0,0,255,255,255]; skips empty leading span, interprets the next lower255 as terminal and submits **no OBJ span at all**. It discards the legal last-pixel interval. The corrected upper-bound termination prevents overrun but does not resolve this boundary/sentinel ambiguity.
- **Evidence boundary:** source-determined skipped draw, not a new emulator/hardware pixel capture. This is directly within the newly integrated H-OBJ implementation, not an unrelated W2/Mode7/general-emulator limitation. CPU startup clears fromF2300 upward; menu/text writes use displayed framebuffer offsets; neither reaches belowF0000. MODE7_TEXTURE154A00, VRAM156A00, palettes166A00/167200, OAM167A00/169C00, sections16C600/171600, caches176600 upward, JIT1C0000 and ROM buffer200000 are disjoint. E90/E94 overlay sources, E98/E9C raw pointers, EA0..F0F seven aligned vectors, F10..F6F padding, F70..FFF resident vectors. **Temporal ownership / RISK:** t5 selects the raw shadow with the same0/4 index as legacy palette/section publication; RSP sp alternates0/4. CPU may build the next side while previous side is rendering; producer must never reuse a raw side until that side's consumer has finished. Current inherited frame synchronization serializes RSP frame reuse and new raw stores are KSEG1 uncached, so no new cache-writeback requirement on those stores. Future RDP raw-palette/TMEM users and RSP readback require an explicit RDP completion boundary: SP HALT or SP DMA idle is not sufficient evidence that RDP output has landed. **Missing raw operands / BLOCKER to a claim of general H-COMP, not a rejection of raw-shadow experiment:**
- CGRAM0 backdrop absent; section MAIN_COLOR and SUB_COLOR still contain quantized brightness-scaled backdrop/fixed color, so inversion of brightness cannot recover raw values.
- Nonzero CGRAM is sampled frame-final; palette writes during a frame lose earlier palette versions. Need preserved palette epochs/deltas or timely raw rendering before mutation; a new section ID alone cannot recover destroyed data.
- BG/OBJ raw pixels require real winner selection and independent Main/Sub designation/windows; one RGB output loses provenance. OBJ color-math eligibility also depends on winning palette (ares DAC::above permits OBJ palette>=192), not just “OBJ won”.
- Direct color for BG1 modes3/4/7 is computed from pixel/palette-group bits, not CGRAM. Mode7 EXTBG needs its own priority/source semantics.
- Sub absence is metadata, not RGB black: a present black pixel differs from transparent subscreen, which falls back to fixed color for math and suppresses HALF when subscreen mode selected. Backdrop on Main has its own CGADSUB bit.
- Pseudo-hires/hires output is interleaved sub/main behavior with cross-pixel DAC state in the pinned reference, not a single256-pixel post-pass. Explicitly outside current proof; do not silently promise it from the two-bit mask.

**Exact semantic order / SUPPORTED by primary reference:** ares17813a3ccda21ab9bd45f09bfc2f91196dbf50ff, ares/sfc/ppu/dac.cpp (above/below/blend/directColor) and window.cpp were read directly. Select winners under layer windows; derive per-winner math eligibility and color-window decisions; clip Main to black when requested **before** math (this is not RGB saturation); decide subscreen vs fixed fallback and effective HALF (suppress for clipped Main, and absent Sub in subscreen mode); apply arithmetic with operation-specific saturation/halving; then master brightness/output. Per channel ADD=min(31,x+y), HALF-ADD=floor((x+y)/2) **before any add saturation**; SUB=max(0,x-y), HALF-SUB=floor(max(0,x-y)/2). Example31+31 half=>31, not15 from saturate-then-half. Prevent-math window can disable arithmetic independently of Main clipping. Pseudo-hires needs the reference's separate below/above ordering. Current kernel implements gated saturating ADD and **fixed coefficient8 output conversion**, not all of those semantics. Hardware E4d authority for its synthetic contract remains closed and is not invalidated by these production requirements.

These are reference implementation evidence, not a new hardware measurement.
